## String References

- **7629**: Trade me a Mog Bonanza prize exchange item and I'll gladly grant you a gorgeous gift of your choosing, kupo!
- **7630**: I'll exchange your $0! Go ahead and select an item from the list of prizes, kupo.
- **7631**: Decided on the prize you'd like to receive? Go ahead and select it from the list, kupo!
- **7632**: What will it be? [Nothing for now./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./$6./Next page./Previous page.]
- **7637**: I'm obliged to offer you a word of warning: there's no going back once I issue you your prize. Are you completely confident about your choice, kupo?
- **7638**: Is this what you really want? Hm? [By Altana, hand it over already!/On second thought...]
- **7639**: I understand. I'll let you hold on to this $3 until you make your decision, kupo.
- **7640**: I understand. Come talk to me again once you've made your decision, kupo.
- **7641**: You're the boss! Here you go, then. Congratulations, kupo!
